& + \; 675,749.02 \cdot \text{beds\_total} \\

& - \; 547,587.42 \cdot \text{area\_id451} \\

& + \; 161,376.52 \cdot \text{area\_id466} \\
& - \; 21,803.63 \cdot \text{area\_id467} \\
& + \; 47,361.13 \cdot \text{area\_id469} \\
& + \; 194,590.77 \cdot \text{area\_id470} \\
& - \; 655,535.19 \cdot \text{area\_id471} \\
& - \; 150,234.81 \cdot \text{area\_id472} \\
& - \; 80,089.67 \cdot \text{area\_id480} \\

\end{array}
